66                   TILE_LOAD_SIZE_3B: load_cmd.byte_count = 3;
                     ==>
67                   TILE_LOAD_SIZE_4B: load_cmd.byte_count = 4;
                     ==>
68                   TILE_LOAD_SIZE_6B: load_cmd.byte_count = 6;
                     ==>
69                   TILE_LOAD_SIZE_8B: load_cmd.byte_count = 8;
                     ==>
70                   TILE_LOAD_SIZE_12B: load_cmd.byte_count = 12;
                     ==>
71                   TILE_LOAD_SIZE_16B: load_cmd.byte_count = 16;
                     ==>
72                   default: ;
                     ==>
73                 endcase
74                 case (tile_loadb_form_e'(b[0][3:1]))
                   -4-  
75                   TILE_LOADB_BITS, TILE_LOADB_BITS_NEAR: begin
76                     load_cmd.form = b[0][1] ? LOAD_BITS_NEAR : LOAD_BITS;
                                               -5-  
                                               ==>  
                                               ==>  
77                     load_cmd.byte_count = 1;
78                     load_cmd.align_new = 0;
79                   end
80                   TILE_LOADB_DATA, TILE_LOADB_DATA_NEAR: begin
81                     load_cmd.form = b[0][1] ? LOAD_DATA_NEAR : LOAD_DATA;
                                               -6-  
                                               ==>  
                                               ==>  
82                     load_cmd.capture_addr = b[1][0];
83                   end
84                   TILE_LOADB_DATA_EXT: begin
85                     load_cmd.byte_count = {1'b0,b[1][7:2]} + 7'd1;
                       ==>
86                     load_cmd.last_bits_m1 = TILE_BYTE_IDX'(TILE_BYTE_WIDTH-1);
87                     load_cmd.capture_addr = b[1][0];
88                   end
89                   TILE_LOADB_INDEX: begin
90                     load_cmd.kind = LOAD_INDEX_VALUE;
                       ==>
91                     load_cmd.byte_count = {5'b0,b[1][7:6]} + 7'd1;
92                   end
93                   default: ;
                     ==>
94                 endcase
95               end
96               TILE_OP_SETM: begin
97                 mm = b[0][3:1];
                   ==>
98                 flag0 = b[0][0];
99                 compute_cmd.execute.params.arg.bit_offset = {2'b0,b[1][7:5]};
100                mask_nibble = b[1][4:1];
101              end
102              TILE_OP_LOGU, TILE_OP_LOGL: begin
103                rb = {2'b0,b[0][3:1]};
104                rc = {2'b0,b[1][7:5]};
105                mm = b[1][4:2];
106                if (tile_opcode_e'(b[0][7:4]) == TILE_OP_LOGU) begin
                   -7-  
107                  compute_cmd.execute.logic_payload.uniform.table_bits = b[2];
                     ==>
108                  compute_cmd.execute.logic_payload.uniform.lane_en = b[3];
109                  flag0 = b[0][0];
110                end else begin
111                  compute_cmd.execute.logic_payload.functions = {b[2],b[3],b[4]};
                     ==>
112                  dst_reg = b[1][0];
113                  rd = b[5][7:3];
114                end
115              end
116              TILE_OP_ST: begin
117                dst_reg = 1;
                   ==>
118                rd = b[1][7:3];
119              end
120              TILE_OP_LDX: begin
121                flag0 = b[0][3];
                   ==>
122                // The five-bit field encodes width-1, not the high endpoint.
123                compute_cmd.execute.params.field_width = {1'b0,b[1][1:0],b[2][7:5]} + $bits(compute_cmd.execute.params.field_width)'(unsigned'(1));
124                compute_cmd.execute.params.arg.bit_offset = b[2][4:0];
125              end
126              TILE_OP_STI: begin
127                dst_reg = 1;
                   ==>
128                rd = b[1][7:3];
129                compute_cmd.execute.params.arg.bit_offset = b[2][7:3];
130              end
131              TILE_OP_ROT: begin
132                ra = {2'b0,b[0][3:1]};
                   ==>
133                compute_cmd.execute.params.arg.bit_offset = b[1][7:3];
134              end
135              TILE_OP_ARITH: begin
136                flag0 = b[0][3];
                   ==>
137                flag1 = b[1][2];
138                compute_cmd.execute.params.group_code = b[0][2:1];
139                mm = b[2][2:0];
140                dst_reg = b[1][1];
141              end
142              TILE_OP_CMP, TILE_OP_SHIFT: begin
143                compute_cmd.execute.params.group_code = b[0][1:0];
                   ==>
144                compute_cmd.execute.params.fn = compute_function_u'(b[0][3:2]);
145                flag0 = b[0][3];
146                flag1 = b[0][2];
147                mm = b[2][2:0];
148                dst_reg = b[1][2];
149              end
150              TILE_OP_RED: begin
151                src_reg = (b[0][3:2] != TILE_RED_ACC_SOURCE);
152                compute_cmd.execute.params.fn = compute_function_u'(src_reg ? b[0][3:2] : b[1][4:3]);
153                compute_cmd.execute.params.field_width = {1'b0,b[0][1:0],b[1][7:5]} + 6'd1;
154                ra = src_reg ? b[1][4:0] : 5'd0;
                                -8-  
                                ==>  
                                ==>  
155              end
156              TILE_OP_IDXR: begin
157                ra = {2'b0,b[0][3:1]};
                   ==>
158                rb = b[1][7:3];
159                flag0 = b[0][0];
160                compute_cmd.execute.params.field_width = 6'd1 << b[1][2:0];
161                compute_cmd.execute.params.arg.bit_offset = {2'b0,b[1][2:0]};
162                rd = ra;
163                dst_reg = b[0][0];
164              end
165              TILE_OP_DESC, TILE_OP_DESC_C: begin
166                commit_req.cmte.command = CMTE_CMD_DESC;
167                commit_req.cmte.amode = b[0][3:2];
168                commit_req.cmte.commit = b[0][7];
169                commit_req.cmte.wide = b[0][1];
170                commit_req.cmte.cell_width_m1 = b[1][7:3];
171                commit_req.cmte.num_cells_m1 = b[1][2:0];
172                if (b[0][0]) begin
                   -9-  
173                  commit_req.cmte.no_compare = b[2][7];
                     ==>
174                  commit_req.cmte.guarded = b[2][6];
175                  commit_req.slot = b[2][5:4];
176                end
                   MISSING_ELSE
                   ==>
177                case (tile_amode_e'(b[0][3:2]))
                   -10-  
178                  TILE_AMODE_CONTIG, TILE_AMODE_INDEXED: begin end
                     ==>
179                  TILE_AMODE_NEAR: begin
180                    commit_req.cmte.desc_address = {{16{descriptor_tail[0][7]}},descriptor_tail[0]};
                       ==>
181                    descriptor_wide_byte = descriptor_tail[1];
182                  end
183                  TILE_AMODE_ABS: begin
184                    commit_req.cmte.desc_address = {descriptor_tail[0],descriptor_tail[1],descriptor_tail[2]};
                       ==>
185                    descriptor_wide_byte = descriptor_tail[3];
186                  end
187                  default: begin end
                     ==>
188                endcase
189                if (b[0][1]) begin
                   -11-  
190                  commit_req.cmte.cell_width_m1 = WORD_IDX'(WORD_WIDTH-1);
                     ==>
191                  commit_req.cmte.num_cells_m1 = '0;
192                  commit_req.cmte.limb_count_m1 = descriptor_wide_byte[7:5];
193                  commit_req.cmte.last_bits_m1 = descriptor_wide_byte[4:0];
194                end
                   MISSING_ELSE
                   ==>
195              end
196              TILE_OP_SYS: case (tile_sys_subop_e'(b[0][3:0]))
                              -12-  
197                TILE_SYS_ENDL: load_cmd.kind = LOAD_END;
                   ==>
198                TILE_SYS_LOADI: begin end
                   ==>
199                TILE_SYS_ENDC: commit_req.cmte.command = CMTE_CMD_ENDC;
                   ==>
200                TILE_SYS_IDXPUSH_C: index_push_cmd.base_address = {b[1],b[2],b[3]};
                   ==>
201                TILE_SYS_IDXPUSH_L: begin
202                  load_cmd.kind = LOAD_PUSH_INDEX_ADDRESS;
                     ==>
203                  load_cmd.address = {b[1],b[2],b[3]};
204                end
205                TILE_SYS_SETC: begin
206                  commit_req.cmte.command = CMTE_CMD_SETC;
                     ==>
207                  commit_req.cmte.cursor_pos = b[1][7:3];
208                end
209                TILE_SYS_LD: begin
210                  ra = b[1][7:3];
                     ==>
211                  flag0 = b[1][2]; // LD.b supplies the commit value/guards.
212                end
213                TILE_SYS_MOV: begin
214                  dst_reg = 1;
                     ==>
215                  rd = b[1][7:3];
216                  ra = b[2][7:3];
217                end
218                TILE_SYS_MUL: begin
219                  dst_reg = b[1][1];
                     ==>
220                  flag0 = b[1][2];
221                end
222                default: ;
                   ==>
223              endcase
224              default: ;
                 ==>

                                                                         -1-

                                                                       -1-

257            case (tile_opcode_e'(op))
               -1-  
258              TILE_OP_LOADB, TILE_OP_SETM, TILE_OP_LOGU, TILE_OP_LOGL,
259              TILE_OP_ST, TILE_OP_DESC, TILE_OP_DESC_C: ; // No result-bearing functional unit.
                 ==>

                 ==>

                 ==>

                              -2-  
                 ==>
                 MISSING_ELSE
                 ==>
265              default: ;

279            case (tile_opcode_e'(op))
               -1-

281                compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
282                compute_cmd.execute.wb.dest_sel = DEST_MASK;
283                compute_cmd.capture.x_sel = X_IMMEDIATE;
284                if (!flag0) begin
                   -2-

                     ==>
286                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.merge_op = MERGE_FIELD;
287                end
                   MISSING_ELSE
                   ==>
288              end

                   ==>

                   ==>

                                                     -4-

                                                     ==>  
322                compute_cmd.execute.wb.result_arithmetic = 1'b1;

                                                           ==>  
                                                           ==>  
325              end
326              TILE_OP_SYS: case (tile_sys_subop_e'(sub))
                              -6-  
327                TILE_SYS_LOADI: begin
328                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
                     ==>
329                  compute_cmd.capture.x_sel = X_IMMEDIATE;
330                end
331                TILE_SYS_LD: begin
332                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
                     ==>

334                  compute_cmd.capture.rs2_sel = TILE_GUARD_REG;
335                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.publish = flag0;
336                end
337                TILE_SYS_MOV: begin
338                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
                     ==>
339                  compute_cmd.capture.x_sel = X_RS1;
340                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.full_mask = 1'b0;
341                end
342                TILE_SYS_MUL: begin
343                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.write_enable = 1'b1;
                     ==>
344                  compute_cmd.capture.x_sel = X_RS2;
345                  compute_cmd.execute.wb.result_arithmetic = 1'b1;
346                end
347                default: ;
                   ==>
348              endcase
349              default: ;
                 ==>

352            if (dispatch.target.load) cmd_o.payload.load.cmd = load_cmd;
               -1-  
               ==>

                    -2-

               ==>

               ==>

Branches:

238                         // Resolve opcode-dependent controls after extracting the instruction fields.
239                         // Runtime execute/start pulses remain the execution controller's responsibility.
240        1/1              compute_cmd.execute.fu.shift_src_x = op == TILE_OP_STI || op == TILE_OP_IDXR;

269                         // An RF destination is read separately after operand capture, before merge.
270        1/1              compute_cmd.capture.rs1_sel = ra;
